                            f"{sum(1 for f in osv_findings if f['sev'] == 'P2')} 个公开已知漏洞，"
                            "建议升级受影响依赖。")
        else:
            warnings.append("ℹ️ OSV 依赖查询完成，未检出已知漏洞（或未发现可查询的依赖清单）。")
    # 零信任清单提示（informational，不影响定级）
    if manifest_missing:
        warnings.append(f"ℹ️ 未发现意图/权限清单（{', '.join(MANIFEST_NAMES)}）。"
                        "借鉴 security-audit-2 的零信任做法，建议要求 Skill 声明明确意图与权限，"
                        "缺失时提升人工复核力度。")
    # 风险评分（0-100）
    risk_score, risk_band = compute_risk_score(tally, len(positive_signals))
    # 准入卡点（v2.5.0 策略引擎：block_at / review_at / network_upload + 信任注册表）
    gate = admission_gate_policy(tally, uploads, POLICY, TRUST, basic["name"])
    eff_profile_name, _ = effective_profile(POLICY, TRUST, basic["name"])
    # 能力画像（安势能力审计：启用后将获得哪些能力）
    capability_profile = build_capability_profile(
        stripped_code + "\n" + body, uploads, tools)
    # 信任认证（借鉴 arc-security-audit）：通过全部高危检查则给出可信基线声明
    attestation = (risk_score >= 90 and tally["P0"] == 0 and tally["P1"] == 0
                   and not obf_hits and not unicode_hits and not dyn_hits and not ioc_hits
                   and not [h for h in obf_artifact_hits if h["sev"] == "P1"])
    # v2.6.0 静态分析局限性声明：当检出混淆/加密特征时，明确警告「真实行为可能被隐藏」
    has_obf_indicators = bool(obf_artifact_hits or obfadv_hits or cmdasm_hits)
    obf_limitation_note = None
    if has_obf_indicators:
        p1_count = sum(1 for h in obf_artifact_hits if h["sev"] == "P1")
        p2_count = sum(1 for h in obf_artifact_hits if h["sev"] == "P2")
        adv_p1 = sum(1 for h in obfadv_hits if h["sev"] == "P1")
        adv_p2 = sum(1 for h in obfadv_hits if h["sev"] == "P2")
        obf_limitation_note = (
            f"⚠️ 静态分析局限性提示：本轮扫描检出混淆/加密相关特征"
            f"（加固/加密文件 {len(obf_artifact_hits)} 条，高熵/分层解码 {len(obfadv_hits)} 条；"
            f"其中 P1×{p1_count + adv_p1} P2×{p2_count + adv_p2}）。"
            "该 Skill 的部分文件已被混淆、加密或高熵打包，其真实行为无法通过静态分析完全确认。"
            "建议：① 人工复核这些命中的具体代码片段；"
            "② 如可能，在隔离环境中动态执行以监控网络/文件/进程行为；"
            "③ 如该 Skill 来自未知来源且叠加了外传/Shell 等危险信号，应极度谨慎。"
        )
    # v2.6.1 补充：语义合规劫持/零载荷攻击的静态局限声明
    sc_p2_count = sum(1 for h in supply_chain_hits if h["sev"] == "P2")
    if supply_chain_hits and obf_limitation_note:
        obf_limitation_note += (
            f"；另检出 {len(supply_chain_hits)} 条 2026 供应链攻击特征"
            f"（其中 P2 语义级 {sc_p2_count} 条，如 SCH/注意力稀释），"
            "语义类攻击（零载荷）本质无法被静态规则可靠捕获，命中仅标记可疑模式，"
            "请务必进行语义复核（人工阅读全文判断指令意图）。"
        )
    # v2.6.0 明确判定 Banner：确保每次扫描都有清晰的最终结论
    grade = grade_letter(risk_score)
    verdict_banner = (f"【最终判定】{gate}｜风险评分 {risk_score}/100（{risk_band}）"
                      f"｜等级 {grade}（{grade_tier(risk_score)}）")
    if has_obf_indicators:
        verdict_banner += "｜⚠️ 存在混淆/加密特征，静态分析覆盖率受限"
    if supply_chain_hits:
        verdict_banner += "｜⚠️ 检出 2026 供应链攻击特征，需人工复核"
    if ransomware_hits:
        verdict_banner += "｜🚨 检出勒索病毒特征，需立即人工复核"

    # v3.0.2 动态沙箱行为检测：真实运行时取证（静态不可见的"按需触发/运行时组装"行为）
    # v3.2.0：记录三态交叉验证——not_applicable(无.py) / clean(有.py无危害) / hit(有动态实证)
    dyn_hits = []
    dyn_summary = {"status": "not_run", "files_probed": 0, "hits": 0}
    if dynamic:
        py_files = [p for p in root.rglob("*.py")
                    if "__pycache__" not in str(p) and not is_self_excluded(p)]
        if not py_files:
            dyn_summary = {"status": "not_applicable", "files_probed": 0, "hits": 0}
            print("动态沙箱检测（--dynamic）：无 .py 脚本，沙箱不适用（无法交叉验证）。", file=sys.stderr)
        else:
            dyn_map = dynamic_probe(root)
            dyn_hits = scan_dynamic_behavior(dyn_map)
            real_hits = [h for h in dyn_hits if "动态实证" in h.get("label", "")]
            dyn_summary = {"status": "hit" if real_hits else "clean",
                           "files_probed": len(dyn_map), "hits": len(dyn_hits),
                           "isolation": dict(_DYNAMIC_ISOLATION)}
            print(f"动态沙箱检测（--dynamic）：隔离后端 = {_DYNAMIC_ISOLATION.get('backend')}"
                  f"（{'硬隔离' if _DYNAMIC_ISOLATION.get('hard') else '非硬隔离'}）；"
                  f"对 {len(dyn_map)} 个 .py 脚本做了执行取证，命中 {len(dyn_hits)} 项运行时行为。",
                  file=sys.stderr)
            if not _DYNAMIC_ISOLATION.get("hard"):
                print(f"  ⚠️ {_DYNAMIC_ISOLATION.get('note')}", file=sys.stderr)
            if dyn_hits:
                merged_hits = list(merged_hits) + dyn_hits  # 动态命中计入风险评分/准入
                has_obf_indicators = has_obf_indicators or any(h["sev"] <= "P1" for h in dyn_hits)

    # 主要功能及范围
    desc = basic["description"]
    headings = mechanisms["headings"]
    scope = {
        "summary": desc,
        "key_sections": headings[:10],
        "capabilities_hint": "、".join(tools.keys()) if tools else "（SKILL.md 中未显式引用工具）",
    }

    result = {
        "skill_path": str(root),
        "basic_info": basic,
        "framework_structure": structure,
        "internal_mechanisms": mechanisms,
        "remote_upload": remote_upload,
        "remote_links": remote_links,
        "security_risks": {"summary": risk_summary, "hits": merged_hits},
        "integrity": {
            "prompt_injection": inject_hits,
            "obfuscation": obf_hits,
            "unicode_threats": unicode_hits,
            "system_impersonation": sysimp_hits,
            "urgent_prepend": urgent_hits,
            "dynamic_context": dyn_hits,
            "consistency": consistency_hits,
            "social_engineering": social_hits,
            "typosquatting": ({"similar_to": ts[0], "ratio": ts[1]} if ts else None),
            "mcp_config": mcp_hits,
            "install_hooks": install_hits,
            "self_cli_theft": selfcli_hits,
            "memory_poisoning": memory_hits,
            "fs_server_abuse": fs_server_hits,
            "binary_assets": binary_hits,
            "taint": taint_hits,
            "taint_ast": taint_ast_hits,
            "obfuscated_payloads": payload_hits,
            "obfuscation_artifacts": obf_artifact_hits,
            "supply_chain_2026": supply_chain_hits,
            "ransomware": ransomware_hits,
            "mcp_sdk_cves": mcp_sdk_hits,
            "selection_manipulation": select_manip_hits,
            "permission_audit": perm_audit_hits,
            "malicious_hashes": malicious_hashes_hits,
            "obfuscation_advanced": obfadv_hits,
            "trigger_conditions": trig_hits,
            "self_integrity": selfint_hits,
            "input_sanitization": sanitize_hits,
            "context_file_tamper": ctx_tamper_hits,
            "self_replication": selfrep_hits,
            "persona_persistence": persona_hits,
            "command_assembly": cmdasm_hits,
            "env_indirection": envind_hits,
            "disguised_filenames": disguise_hits,
            "dynamic_behavior": dyn_hits,
            # v3.2.5 新增：文件膨胀/填充规避 + 不安全反序列化 RCE + 代理级金融欺诈诱导 + 浏览器绕过
            "file_inflation": inflate_hits,
            "unsafe_deserialization": deser_hits,
            "agentic_fraud": fraud_hits,
            "browser_evasion": browser_hits,
            # v3.2.6 新增：抗绕过归一化重扫 + 图片元数据注入 + 依赖源重定向
            "evasion_normalized": evasion_hits,
            "image_metadata_injection": imgmeta_hits,
            "dependency_redirection": depredir_hits,
            # v3.2.8 情报补强：标准库同名模块劫持 / Agent 配置文件投毒
            "module_shadowing": modshadow_hits,
            "agent_config_poison": agentcfg_hits,
        },
        "dynamic_summary": dyn_summary,
        "reputation": reputation,
        "review_queue": review_queue,
        "functions_and_scope": scope,
        "positive_signals": positive_signals,
        "manifest_missing": manifest_missing,
        "owasp_agentic": owasp_agentic,
        "capability_profile": capability_profile,
        "admission_gate": gate,
        "risk_score": {"score": risk_score, "band": risk_band},
        "attestation": "通过静态安全审计（可信基线，仍建议定期复查）" if attestation
                       else "未通过（存在需关注的命中）",
        "verdict": verdict,
        "verdict_banner": verdict_banner,
        "grade": grade,
        "grade_tier": grade_tier(risk_score),
        "warnings": warnings,
        "signals": signals,
        # 真实计分 tally（即 compute_risk_score / admission_gate 实际使用的严重度计数，
        # 与 security_risks.summary 同源但不去重，供调用方核对评分一致性）。
        "severity_tally": tally,
        "obfuscation_warning": obf_limitation_note,
        "nosec": {"applied": bool(nosec_cfg and getattr(nosec_cfg, "loaded", False)),
                  "suppressed": suppressed},
        "osv_findings": osv_findings,
        "policy_active": eff_profile_name,
        "rule_pack": {"applied": list(_RULE_PACK_STATUS["applied"]),
                      "skipped": list(_RULE_PACK_STATUS["skipped"])},
        # v3.3.6 OBS-1（REDTEAM_336）：统一发现集——integrity 命名空间的命中此前不进
        # security_risks.hits/summary，下游 CI/报表会系统性低估。此字段聚合全部模块命中
        # （去重、按严重度排序、标注来源），评分/tally 口径不变（避免双重计分回归）。
    }
    result["unified_findings"] = _unified_findings(merged_hits, result.get("integrity", {}))
    return result


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 防范与案例（供报告「防范建议」章节使用）
PREVENTION_MEASURES = [
    "沙箱隔离执行：在隔离容器/受限环境中运行 Skill，仅挂载任务所需目录，非必要时禁用出网，"
    "对无需写入处设为只读文件系统。",

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 批量扫描（Mode A）
def find_skills(root: Path):
    """在 root 下找出所有含 SKILL.md 的技能目录（支持嵌套一层）。"""
    skills = []
    if (root / "SKILL.md").exists():
        skills.append(root)
    for p in sorted(root.rglob("SKILL.md")):
        d = p.parent
        if d not in skills:
            skills.append(d)
    return skills


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- v3.3.2 批量并行（进程级，保守）
_BATCH_PARALLEL_MIN = 11       # 仅目标数 > 10 才启用并行（≤10 恒串行）
_BATCH_MAX_WORKERS = 4         # 并发上限
_BATCH_SUBPROC_TIMEOUT = 600   # 单个子进程超时（秒）


def batch_workers(n_targets: int) -> int:
    """批量并发度（保守策略）：目标数 ≤10 → 1（串行）；否则 min(4, cpu_count//2)。

    可用环境变量 SKAUDIT_BATCH_WORKERS 显式覆盖（1 = 强制串行；用于对照/回归测试）。
    """
    env = os.environ.get("SKAUDIT_BATCH_WORKERS")
    if env:
        try:
            return max(1, min(_BATCH_MAX_WORKERS, int(env)))
        except ValueError:
            pass
    if n_targets < _BATCH_PARALLEL_MIN:
        return 1
    cpu = os.cpu_count() or 2
    return max(1, min(_BATCH_MAX_WORKERS, cpu // 2))


def _norm_sandbox_paths(events, workdir: Path):
    """把动态事件中的隔离工作目录前缀替换为稳定占位符 `<sandbox>`。

    v3.3.2：隔离 workdir 由 `tempfile` 随机生成，若原样写入报告会导致**同一目标两次
    扫描输出不一致**（报告不可复现）。归一化后报告可逐字节复现。
    """
    if not events:
        return events
    variants = set()
    for wd in (workdir,):
        try:
            variants |= {str(wd), str(wd).replace("\\", "/")}
            rp = wd.resolve()
            variants |= {str(rp), str(rp).replace("\\", "/")}
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
            pass
    variants = sorted((v for v in variants if v), key=len, reverse=True)
    out = []
    for ev in events:
        if isinstance(ev, (list, tuple)):
            ev = [x for x in ev]
            for i, x in enumerate(ev):
                if isinstance(x, str):
                    for v in variants:
                        if v in x:
                            x = x.replace(v, "<sandbox>")
                    ev[i] = x
            out.append(tuple(ev))
        else:
            out.append(ev)
    return out


def _entry_script():
    """定位进程级并行所需的子进程入口（scripts/sandbox_run.py）；找不到则回退串行。"""
    cands = []
    root = os.environ.get("SKAUDIT_ROOT")
    if root:
        cands.append(Path(root) / "scripts" / "sandbox_run.py")
    try:
        cands.append(Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "sandbox_run.py")
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
        pass
    for c in cands:
        try:
            if c.is_file():
                return c
        except OSError:
            continue
    return None


def _batch_err_result(skill_dir, msg: str):
    """并行子进程侧的失败结果（带哨兵键，供 analyze_dir 判定是否整体回退串行）。"""
    return {"error": f"{msg}（{skill_dir}）", "skill_path": str(skill_dir),
            "admission_gate": "ERROR（无法完成扫描）", "warnings": [msg],
            "_batch_error": True,
            "basic_info": {"name": Path(str(skill_dir)).name}}


def _analyze_subprocess(skill_dir, pass_args):
    """在**独立子进程**中扫描单个 Skill（进程级隔离）。

    返回结果 dict；无法完成时返回 error 结果（调用方据此回退串行）。
    """
    entry = _entry_script()
    if entry is None:
        return None  # 交由 analyze_dir 串行回退
    cmd = [sys.executable, str(entry), str(skill_dir)] + list(pass_args) + ["--json"]
    try:
        p = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8",
                           errors="replace", timeout=_BATCH_SUBPROC_TIMEOUT)
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        return _batch_err_result(skill_dir, "子进程扫描超时")
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _batch_err_result(skill_dir, f"子进程启动失败：{exc}")
    out = (p.stdout or "").strip()
    if not out:
        return _batch_err_result(skill_dir, f"子进程无输出（exit={p.returncode}）")
    try:
        res = json.loads(out)
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _batch_err_result(skill_dir, "子进程输出无法解析为 JSON")
    if not isinstance(res, dict):
        return _batch_err_result(skill_dir, "子进程输出结构非预期")
    return res


def analyze_dir_simple(root: Path, osv: bool = False):
    """纯串行批量分析（保留旧行为，供并行不可用时回退与对照测试使用）。"""
    return [analyze(s, osv=osv) for s in find_skills(root)]


def analyze_dir(root: Path, osv: bool = False, passthrough=None, dynamic: bool = False):
    """批量分析目录下的多个 Skill，返回结果列表（**顺序恒与 find_skills 一致**）。

    v3.3.2：目标数 > 10 且未启用动态取证时，改用**进程级并行**（独立子进程，天然隔离
    模块级全局状态）以加速；任何子任务失败或子进程入口不可用 → **整体自动回退串行**，
    结果顺序与串行完全一致（保证报告可复现）。
    """
    skills = find_skills(root)
    pass_args = list(passthrough or [])
    if osv and "--osv" not in pass_args:
        pass_args.append("--osv")
    workers = 1 if dynamic else batch_workers(len(skills))
    if workers <= 1 or _entry_script() is None:
        return [analyze(s, osv=osv) for s in skills]
    import concurrent.futures as _cf
    results = [None] * len(skills)
    try:
        with _cf.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=workers) as ex:
            # 线程仅用于「等待子进程」，实际扫描在各独立子进程内完成（进程级隔离）
            futs = {ex.submit(_analyze_subprocess, s, pass_args): i
                    for i, s in enumerate(skills)}
            for fut in _cf.as_completed(futs):
                results[futs[fut]] = fut.result()
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
        return [analyze(s, osv=osv) for s in skills]
    if any(r is None for r in results):
        return [analyze(s, osv=osv) for s in skills]
    if any(isinstance(r, dict) and r.get("_batch_error") for r in results):
        # 任一子任务失败 → 整体回退串行，保证结果可信且与串行一致
        return [analyze(s, osv=osv) for s in skills]
    return results


def auto_dynamic_review(results, osv: bool = False):
    """v3.2.0：批量扫描后对高危(P0/P1) Skill 自动启用动态沙箱取证，不再需用户手动选择。

    对每个含 P0/P1 命中的 Skill 重跑 analyze(dynamic=True)，动态命中自动计入风险评分/准入/判定。
    无 .py 脚本的 Skill 沙箱自然返回空（不适用），仅重扫一次，成本可控。
    """
    sev_rank = {"P0": 0, "P1": 1, "P2": 2, "P3": 3}
    for i, r in enumerate(results):
        if "error" in r:
            continue
        worst = min((sev_rank.get(h.get("sev"), 9) for h in collect_all_hits(r)), default=9)
        if worst > 1:  # 仅 P0/P1 视为高危，自动沙箱
            continue
        root = Path(r.get("skill_path", ""))
        if not root.is_dir():
            continue
        print(f"🔬 自动沙箱复查高危 Skill：{r['basic_info']['name']}（{root}）", file=sys.stderr)
        results[i] = analyze(root, osv=osv, dynamic=True)
    return results


def _sev_key(r):
    """按安全等级生成排序键：高危(P0>P1>P2>P3)置顶，失败置顶，干净垫底。"""
    if "error" in r:
        return (-2, 0, 0, 0, 0)
    t = r.get("severity_tally", {})
    p0 = int(t.get("P0", 0)); p1 = int(t.get("P1", 0))
    p2 = int(t.get("P2", 0)); p3 = int(t.get("P3", 0))
    if p0 or p1:
        return (-1, -p0, -p1, -p2, -p3)
    if p2 or p3:
        return (0, -p2, -p3, 0, 0)
    return (1, 0, 0, 0, 0)


def render_summary_table(results, source_fn=lambda r: "本地"):
    """批量扫描汇总表：高危 Skill 置顶，按安全等级(P0>P1>P2>P3)排序。

    v3.2.0：报告头部标注 skill-analysis 版本；新增 P0/P1/P2/P3 严重度计数列，
    排序从「仅按判定标签」升级为「按安全等级计数降序」，高危置顶。
    """
    rows = []
    failed = 0
    for r in results:
        if "error" in r:
            failed += 1
            rows.append({"name": str(r.get("skill_path", "?")), "src": source_fn(r),
                         "verdict": "❌ 扫描失败", "tally": {}, "key": (-2, 0, 0, 0, 0)})
            continue
        rows.append({"name": r["basic_info"]["name"], "src": source_fn(r),
                     "verdict": verdict_label(r), "tally": r.get("severity_tally", {}),
                     "key": _sev_key(r)})
    rows.sort(key=lambda x: x["key"])
    L = [f"## 🔍 Skill 安全扫描结果\n",
         f"> 审计器：skill-analysis v{__version__}｜共扫描 {len(rows)} 个 Skill（高危置顶，按安全等级排序）\n"]
    L.append("| # | Skill 名称 | 来源 | P0 | P1 | P2 | P3 | 检测结果 |")
    L.append("|---|-----------|------|----|----|----|----|---------|")
    for i, r in enumerate(rows, 1):
        t = r["tally"]
        L.append(f"| {i} | {r['name']} | {r['src']} | {t.get('P0', 0)} | {t.get('P1', 0)} "
                 f"| {t.get('P2', 0)} | {t.get('P3', 0)} | {r['verdict']} |")
    ok = len(rows) - failed
    L.append(f"\n**结论**：成功扫描 {ok} 个，失败 {failed} 个。"
             + ("有 Skill 扫描失败，请查看其错误详情后再做准入决定。" if failed else "全部扫描完成。"))
    return "\n".join(L)


def render_nine_dim_table(results, source_fn=lambda r: "本地"):
    """批量模式「9 维深度报告」表格：每个 Skill 一行，9 个维度各一列（表格化，不简单罗列）。

    9 维对齐单 Skill 报告的 9 个章节：①信息 ②框架结构 ③内部机制 ④远程上传
    ⑤远程链接 ⑥安全风险 ⑦指令完整性 ⑧功能范围 ⑨结论。高危置顶按安全等级排序。
    """
    rows = []
    for r in results:
        if "error" in r:
            rows.append({"name": str(r.get("skill_path", "?")), "src": source_fn(r),
                         "v": "—", "s": "—", "m": "—", "u": "扫描失败", "l": "—",
                         "risk": "—", "i": "—", "f": "—", "g": "❌ 扫描失败",
                         "key": (-2, 0, 0, 0, 0)})
            continue
        b = r.get("basic_info", {}); s = r.get("framework_structure", {})
        me = r.get("internal_mechanisms", {}); ru = r.get("remote_upload", {})
        sr = r.get("security_risks", {}).get("summary", {})
        sc = r.get("functions_and_scope", {})
        t = r.get("severity_tally", {})
        # ⑦ 指令完整性命中计数：integrity 内所有 list 命中项之和
        icount = sum(len(v) for v in r.get("integrity", {}).values()
                     if isinstance(v, list))
        risk_txt = f"{t.get('P0', 0)}/{t.get('P1', 0)}/{t.get('P2', 0)}/{t.get('P3', 0)}"
        rows.append({
            "name": b.get("name", "?"), "src": source_fn(r),
            "v": b.get("version", "?"),
            "s": f"{s.get('file_count', '?')}f" + ("/scr" if s.get("has_scripts") else ""),
            "m": me.get("skill_type", "?"),
            "u": ru.get("verdict", "?"),
            "l": str(len(r.get("remote_links", []))),
            "risk": risk_txt, "i": str(icount),
            "f": (sc.get("capabilities_hint") or "—")[:20],
            "g": r.get("verdict", "?"),
            "key": _sev_key(r),
        })
    rows.sort(key=lambda x: x["key"])
    L = [f"### 9 维深度报告（表格）\n",
         f"> 审计器：skill-analysis v{__version__}｜列含义：①版本 ②文件数/脚本 ③类型 ④远程上传 ⑤链接数 "
         f"⑥风险P0/P1/P2/P3 ⑦完整性命中 ⑧功能范围 ⑨结论\n"]
    L.append("| Skill | 来源 | ①信息 | ②结构 | ③机制 | ④上传 | ⑤链接 | ⑥风险 | ⑦完整性 | ⑧范围 | ⑨结论 |")
    L.append("|-------|------|------|------|------|------|------|------|--------|------|------|")
    for r in rows:
        L.append(f"| {r['name']} | {r['src']} | {r['v']} | {r['s']} | {r['m']} | {r['u']} | "
                 f"{r['l']} | {r['risk']} | {r['i']} | {r['f']} | {r['g']} |")
    return "\n".join(L)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 渲染
# v3.2.11 安全加固：Markdown 报告字段净化——被审计 Skill 的 description/snippet/文件名等
# 属不可信内容，直接 f-string 插值会原样进报告；若报告在支持 HTML/链接的渲染器打开，
# 可能被诱导渲染（潜在注入面）。_md_safe 剔除控制字符（保留 \n\t）、转义 HTML 尖括号，
# 并把行内片段截断，降低注入/体积滥用；JSON 结果保持原始，供机器消费。
_MD_CTRL = re.compile(r"[\x00-\x08\x0b\x0c\x0e-\x1f\x7f]")
def _md_safe(t, limit=400):
    if t is None:
        return ""
    t = str(t)
    t = _MD_CTRL.sub("", t)
    t = t.replace("&", "&amp;").replace("<", "&lt;").replace(">", "&gt;")
    if limit and len(t) > limit:
        t = t[:limit] + "…"
    return t


# v3.3.5 竞品采纳（对标 security-sweep / security-scan 的 per-finding Action 设计）：
# 按命中类别给出**修复建议（remediation）**，渲染进报告，弥补「只判问题、不给处置」的短板。
_REMEDIATION_TABLE = (
    (("密钥", "凭据", "secret", "SECRET", "token", "Token", "密码", "私钥", "JWT"),
     "移除硬编码密钥并**轮换已可能泄露的凭据**；改用环境变量或密钥管理服务注入。"),
    (("eval", "exec", "动态执行", "动态求值", "pickle", "marshal", "反序列化"),
     "移除动态执行/不安全反序列化；确需动态能力时改用显式分发表并白名单校验输入。"),
    (("下载执行", "curl", "wget", "远程执行", "管道执行"),
     "不要管道执行远程脚本；改为下载后校验哈希/签名，或用包管理器安装固定版本。"),
    (("外传", "上传", "POST", "外发", "数据回传", "回传"),
     "明确数据用途并征得用户同意；外发目标域名加入可信清单并在 SKILL.md 声明。"),
    (("环境变量", "envsteal", "env 泄露"),
     "仅读取功能声明所需的环境变量；禁止把 env 值拼进命令行或外发请求。"),
    (("rm -rf", "删除", "擦除"),
     "把删除目标限定在白名单路径内，删除前增加二次确认与日志。"),
    (("提示注入", "忽略", "覆盖指令", "冒充", "角色劫持"),
     "删除对抗性指令文本；用声明式约束替代命令式操控，正文与实际行为保持一致。"),
    (("混淆", "加固", "加密壳", "PyArmor", "签名验签"),
     "移除商业混淆与自实现密码学，提供明文源码与标准依赖供审计。"),
    (("MCP", "工具投毒", "工具描述", "tool description"),
     "工具描述与实现保持一致并纳入版本签名；删除未声明的描述改写与运行时元数据变更。"),
    (("反分析", "反调试", "检测对抗", "侦测"),
     "移除反调试/反分析对抗代码；审计工具对透明实现不做惩罚。"),
    (("路径穿越", "symlink", "符号链接", "任意文件"),
     "对输入路径做规范化并限制在声明的目标目录内。"),
    (("监听", "绑定", "0.0.0.0", "::", "无鉴权"),
     "服务只绑定回环地址（127.0.0.1），必须对外时增加鉴权中间件与访问控制。"),
)


def _remediation_for(hit):
    """按命中标签/描述关键词给出修复建议（竞品采纳：对标 per-finding Action）。"""
    text = f"{hit.get('label', '')} {hit.get('why', '')} {hit.get('snippet', '')}"
    for keys, advice in _REMEDIATION_TABLE:
        if any(k in text for k in keys):
            return advice
    return ("对照命中描述核实该行为的必要性：无法说明合法用途的，按最坏假设处置"
            "（移除该能力或在隔离环境运行）。")


def render_markdown(r):
    if "error" in r:
        # v2.6.2：错误结果也渲染明确判定，而非裸错误文本
        return (f"# Skill 安全与结构分析报告：扫描失败\n\n"
                f"**【最终判定】{r.get('admission_gate', 'ERROR（无法完成扫描）')}**\n\n"
                f"❌ {r['error']}\n\n"
                + ("\n".join(f"- {w}" for w in r.get("warnings", [])) if r.get("warnings") else "")
                + "\n\n> 提示：确认目标路径为包含 SKILL.md 的有效 Skill 目录，或查看 `references/troubleshooting.md` 的 Q1 故障排查。")
    b = r["basic_info"]
    s = r["framework_structure"]
    mech = r["internal_mechanisms"]
    ru = r["remote_upload"]
    sr = r["security_risks"]
    sc = r["functions_and_scope"]

    L = []
    L.append(f"# Skill 安全与结构分析报告：{_md_safe(b['name'])}\n")
    L.append(f"> 审计器：skill-analysis v{__version__}　本报告仅供审计参考。\n")
    L.append("> 依据标签：`[静态扫描]`=规则/正则命中　`[动态沙箱]`=隔离执行取证　`[推断]`=模式推断（非实证）；"
             "无法判定的维度将明确标注，不臆测编造。\n")

    # 1. 基本信息
    L.append("## 1. 基本信息")
    L.append(f"- **名称**：{_md_safe(b['name'])}")
    L.append(f"- **描述**：{_md_safe(b['description'], 600)}")
    L.append(f"- **版本**：{_md_safe(b['version'])}")
    L.append(f"- **许可证**：{_md_safe(b['license'])}")
    pub = b["publisher"]
    L.append("- **发行人/作者**：" + (", ".join(f"{k}={v}" for k, v in pub) if pub else "未声明"))
    if b.get("frontmatter_perms"):
        perms = b["frontmatter_perms"]
        L.append("- **frontmatter 权限声明**：" +
                 "；".join(f"{k}={v}" for k, v in perms.items()))
    else:
        L.append("- **frontmatter 权限声明**：（未声明，将以会话默认权限运行）")

    # 2. 框架结构
    L.append("\n## 2. 框架结构")
    L.append(f"- **文件总数**：{s['file_count']}")
    L.append(f"- **scripts/**：{'有' if s['has_scripts'] else '无'}　"
             f"**references/**：{'有' if s['has_references'] else '无'}　"
             f"**assets/**：{'有' if s['has_assets'] else '无'}")
    if s["script_langs"]:
        L.append(f"- **脚本语言**：{s['script_langs']}")
    if s["text_files"]:
        L.append("- **文本文件**：" + "、".join(s["text_files"][:15]) +
                 (" …" if len(s["text_files"]) > 15 else ""))
    if s["other_files"]:
        L.append("- **其他文件**：" + "、".join(s["other_files"][:10]))

    # 3. 内部机制
    L.append("\n## 3. 内部机制")
    L.append(f"- **推断类型**：{mech['skill_type']}（评分 {mech['skill_type_score']}）")
    if mech["tools_referenced"]:
        L.append("- **引用的工具**：" +
                 "、".join(f"{k}({v})" for k, v in mech["tools_referenced"].items()))
    else:
        L.append("- **引用的工具**：（SKILL.md 中未显式引用内置工具）")
    L.append(f"- **调用脚本**：{'是' if mech['uses_scripts'] else '否'}　"
             f"**调用参考文档**：{'是' if mech['uses_references'] else '否'}　"
             f"**使用素材**：{'是' if mech['uses_assets'] else '否'}")

    # 4. 远程上传
    L.append("\n## 4. 是否远程上传内容")
    L.append(f"- **结论**：{ru['verdict']}")
    if ru["upload_signals"]:
        L.append("- **数据外传特征**：")
        for label, snip in ru["upload_signals"]:
            L.append(f"  - {label}：`{snip}`")
    if ru["readonly_signals"]:
        L.append("- **只读网络请求**：")
        for label, snip in ru["readonly_signals"][:8]:
            L.append(f"  - {label}：`{snip}`")

    # 5. 远程链接
    L.append("\n## 5. 远程链接地址分析")
    if r["remote_links"]:
        L.append(f"- 共发现 {len(r['remote_links'])} 个 URL：")
        for link in r["remote_links"]:
            flag = " ⚠️疑似上传/上报端点" if link["upload_like"] else ""
            L.append(f"  - `{link['url']}` （域名 {link['domain']}，文件：{', '.join(link['files'])}）{flag}")
    else:
        L.append("- 未发现远程链接。")

    # 6. 安全风险（命令级危险模式 + 凭据读取 + 平台威胁 + IOC）
    L.append("\n## 6. 安全风险评估（命令级 + 凭据访问 + 平台威胁 + IOC）")
    summ = sr["summary"]
    L.append(f"- **风险分布**：P0(严重)×{summ['P0']}　P1(高)×{summ['P1']}　"
             f"P2(中)×{summ['P2']}　P3(低)×{summ['P3']}")
    if sr["hits"]:
        L.append("| 等级 | 命中项 | 位置 | 片段 | 影响与建议 |")
        L.append("|------|--------|------|------|-----------|")
        for h in sr["hits"]:
            impact, rec = guidance_for(h["label"], h["sev"])
            plain = plain_language(h["label"], h["sev"])
            # v3.2.11：片段/标签属不可信内容，净化后再入表格单元格（防 HTML/控制字符注入报告渲染）
            snip = _md_safe(str(h.get("snippet", ""))).replace("|", "\\|").replace("\n", " ").replace("\r", "")
            lbl = _md_safe(h["label"]).replace("|", "\\|")
            tail = f"影响：{impact}；建议：{rec}"
            if plain:
                tail += f"；风险提示：{plain}"
            L.append(f"| {h['sev']} | {lbl} | 行{h.get('line', 0)} | `{snip}` | {tail} |")
    else:
        L.append("- 未检出已知危险命令模式。")

    # 7. 指令完整性与提示注入
    L.append("\n## 7. 指令完整性 / 提示注入 / 隐蔽编码 / 权限劫持")
    integ = r["integrity"]
    any_integ = False
    if integ["prompt_injection"]:
        any_integ = True
        L.append("- **提示注入 / 越权指令**：")
        for h in integ["prompt_injection"]:
            impact, rec = guidance_for(h["label"], h["sev"])
            L.append(f"  - **[{h['sev']}] {h['label']}**（SKILL.md 行 {h['line']}）：`{h['snippet']}`")
            L.append(f"    - 🎯 影响范围：{impact}　💡 建议：{rec}")
            if not h.get("doc_exempt"):
                L.append(f"    - 🛠 修复建议：{_remediation_for(h)}")
    if integ["obfuscation"]:
        any_integ = True
        L.append("- **隐蔽编码**：")
        for h in integ["obfuscation"]:
            impact, rec = guidance_for(h["label"], h["sev"])
            L.append(f"  - **[{h['sev']}] {h['label']}**（{h['snippet']}）")
            L.append(f"    - 🎯 影响范围：{impact}　💡 建议：{rec}")
    if integ.get("unicode_threats"):
        any_integ = True
        L.append("- **Unicode 走私 / 同形字**：")
        for h in integ["unicode_threats"]:
            impact, rec = guidance_for(h["label"], h["sev"])
            L.append(f"  - **[{h['sev']}] {h['label']}**（{h['snippet']}）")
            L.append(f"    - 🎯 影响范围：{impact}　💡 建议：{rec}")
    if integ.get("system_impersonation"):
        any_integ = True
        L.append("- **系统消息/角色冒充**：")
        for h in integ["system_impersonation"]:
            impact, rec = guidance_for(h["label"], h["sev"])
            L.append(f"  - **[{h['sev']}] {h['label']}**（SKILL.md 行 {h['line']}）：`{h['snippet']}`")
            L.append(f"    - 🎯 影响范围：{impact}　💡 建议：{rec}")
    if integ.get("urgent_prepend"):
        any_integ = True
        L.append("- **前置强制指令**：")
        for h in integ["urgent_prepend"]:
            impact, rec = guidance_for(h["label"], h["sev"])
            L.append(f"  - **[{h['sev']}] {h['label']}**（SKILL.md 行 {h['line']}）：`{h['snippet']}`")
            L.append(f"    - 🎯 影响范围：{impact}　💡 建议：{rec}")
    if integ["dynamic_context"]:
        any_integ = True
        L.append("- **动态上下文 Shell 执行**：")
        for h in integ["dynamic_context"]:
            impact, rec = guidance_for(h["label"], h["sev"])
            L.append(f"  - **[{h['sev']}]**（行 {h['line']}）：`{h['snippet']}`")
            L.append(f"    - 🎯 影响范围：{impact}　💡 建议：{rec}")
    if integ["consistency"]:
        any_integ = True
        for h in integ["consistency"]:
            L.append(f"  - **[{h['sev']}] {h['label']}**：{h['why']}")
            L.append(f"        ↳ 修复建议：{_remediation_for(h)}")
    if integ["typosquatting"]:
        any_integ = True
        ts = integ["typosquatting"]
        L.append(f"- **错别字抢注风险**：名称与已知 Skill `{ts['similar_to']}` "
                 f"相似度 {ts['ratio']}，请核对发布者是否为官方。")
    if integ.get("social_engineering"):
        any_integ = True
        L.append("- **社会工程学话术（提示）**：")
        for h in integ["social_engineering"]:
            impact, rec = guidance_for(h["label"], h["sev"])
            L.append(f"  - **[{h['sev']}] {h['label']}**（SKILL.md 行 {h['line']}）：`{h['snippet']}`")
            L.append(f"    - 🎯 影响范围：{impact}　💡 建议：{rec}")

    # 7.6 v2.4.0 新增 Agent 生态攻击面（MCP 配置劫持 / 供应链安装钩子 / 自 CLI 窃密 /
    # 记忆篡改 / 文件系统&本地服务滥用 / 二进制素材未扫描）
    def _render_block(title, hits):
        if not hits:
            return False
        any_integ = True
        L.append(f"- **{title}**：")
        for h in hits:
            impact, rec = guidance_for(h["label"], h["sev"])
            loc = f"（SKILL.md 行 {h['line']}）" if h.get("line") else ""
            L.append(f"  - **[{h['sev']}] {h['label']}**{loc}：`{h['snippet']}`")
            L.append(f"    - 🎯 影响范围：{impact}　💡 建议：{rec}")
        return True

    _render_block("MCP 配置劫持 / 工具投毒信号", integ.get("mcp_config", []))
    _render_block("供应链安装钩子 / 安装源风险", integ.get("install_hooks", []))
    _render_block("勒索病毒特征（v3.2.3：赎金勒索信/文件加密/删除卷影副本/家族名/后缀）", integ.get("ransomware", []))
    _render_block("借用 agent 自身 CLI 窃密", integ.get("self_cli_theft", []))
    _render_block("Agent 持久记忆篡改", integ.get("memory_poisoning", []))
    _render_block("文件系统滥用 / 本地无鉴权服务", integ.get("fs_server_abuse", []))
    # 污点链命中已在第 6 节（security_risks.hits）展示，此处不重复渲染；机器可读仍保留
    _render_block("多层混淆载荷（v2.5.3）", integ.get("obfuscated_payloads", []))
    if integ.get("binary_assets"):
        any_integ = True
        L.append(f"- **未扫描的二进制素材（{len(integ['binary_assets'])} 个）**：")
        for h in integ["binary_assets"]:
            L.append(f"  - **[{h['sev']}] {h['label']}**：{h['why']}")
            L.append(f"        ↳ 修复建议：{_remediation_for(h)}")
    # v2.6.3 新增：权限过度授权审计 + 已知恶意 Skill 指纹库
    _render_block("权限过度授权审计（v2.6.3）", integ.get("permission_audit", []))
    _render_block("已知恶意 Skill 指纹命中（v2.6.3）", integ.get("malicious_hashes", []))
    # v3.0.0 新增：增强混淆识别 / 触发条件检测 / 自身防护
    _render_block("增强混淆识别（v3.0.0：高熵文件 / 跨语言分层解码执行）", integ.get("obfuscation_advanced", []))
    _render_block("高级恶意行为触发条件（v3.0.0：env/time/host/debug/argv 门控）", integ.get("trigger_conditions", []))
    _render_block("分析器自身完整性自检（v3.0.0：防篡改）", integ.get("self_integrity", []))
    _render_block("输入净化（v3.0.0：源码含 NUL 字节检测）", integ.get("input_sanitization", []))
    # v3.0.1 新增：持久化上下文篡改 / 自传播 / 混淆组装 / env 间接执行 / 伪装文件名
    _render_block("持久化上下文文件篡改（v3.0.1：写 SOUL/AGENTS/HEARTBEAT 等）", integ.get("context_file_tamper", []))
    _render_block("自传播/写入技能目录（v3.0.1：语义蠕虫）", integ.get("self_replication", []))
    _render_block("自改写/身份持久化（v3.3.1：Persona Persistence）", integ.get("persona_persistence", []))
    _render_block("AST 污点链（v3.3.2：跨语句 source→sink 数据流）", integ.get("taint_ast", []))
    _render_block("命令拼接/转义编码混淆（v3.0.1）", integ.get("command_assembly", []))
    _render_block("环境变量间接执行（v3.0.1）", integ.get("env_indirection", []))
    _render_block("伪装文件名（v3.0.1）", integ.get("disguised_filenames", []))
    # v3.2.0：动态沙箱三态交叉验证结论（hit=实证 / clean=适用无危害 / not_applicable=无.py不适用）
    _dyn_sum = r.get("dynamic_summary", {})
    _dyn_status = _dyn_sum.get("status", "not_run")
    _dyn_behavior = integ.get("dynamic_behavior", [])
    if _dyn_status == "hit":
        _render_block("动态沙箱行为取证（--dynamic 隔离子进程+审计钩子）", _dyn_behavior)
    elif _dyn_status == "clean":
        any_integ = True
        L.append(f"- **动态沙箱取证（自动复查）** [动态沙箱]：已对 {_dyn_sum.get('files_probed', 0)} 个 .py 脚本隔离执行，"
                 f"未发现运行时危险行为——弱佐证（不排除「按需触发」型危险），风险等级维持静态判定，需结合功能人工复核。")
    elif _dyn_status == "not_applicable":
        any_integ = True
        L.append("- **动态沙箱取证（自动复查）** [动态沙箱]：本 Skill 无 .py 脚本，沙箱不适用——"
                 "交叉验证无信息，风险等级维持静态判定，需人工复核意图。")

    if not any_integ:
        L.append("- 未检出提示注入、隐蔽编码、Unicode 走私、权限劫持、动态上下文、社会工程学话术、"
                 "名字抢注、MCP 配置劫持、供应链安装钩子、自 CLI 窃密、记忆篡改或文件系统/本地服务滥用特征。")

    # 7.5 能力画像 + 准入卡点（安势能力审计 / 三道防线）
    L.append("\n## 7.5 能力画像与准入卡点")
    caps = r.get("capability_profile", [])
    if caps:
        L.append("- **能力画像（启用后获得的敏感能力）**：" + "、".join(caps) +
                 "\n  （供最小权限配置使用：把「可以做」收敛到「需要做」）")
    else:
        L.append("- **能力画像**：未检出敏感能力。")
    L.append(f"- **准入卡点**：{r.get('admission_gate', '—')}"
             "（BLOCK=禁止准入 / NEED_REVIEW=需人工复核 / REVIEW=建议关注 / PASS=可准入）")

    # 8. 主要功能及范围
    L.append("\n## 8. 主要功能及范围")
    L.append(f"- **摘要**：{sc['summary']}")
    if sc["key_sections"]:
        L.append("- **核心章节**：" + "、".join(sc["key_sections"][:10]))
    L.append(f"- **能力线索（工具）**：{sc['capabilities_hint']}")

    # 9. 总体结论 + 防范建议
    L.append("\n## 9. 总体结论与防范建议")
    # v2.6.0 明确判定 Banner：报告最显眼位置输出最终判定（JSON 与 Markdown 一致）
    if r.get("verdict_banner"):
        L.append(f"### {r['verdict_banner']}\n")
    L.append(f"- **综合评级**：{r['verdict']}")
    rs = r.get("risk_score", {})
    L.append(f"- **风险评分**：{rs.get('score', '?')}/100（{rs.get('band', '?')}）"
             f"　（借鉴 skill-security-auditor 的风险分，越高越安全）")
    L.append(f"- **发布建议等级**：{r.get('grade', '?')}（{r.get('grade_tier', '')}）"
             f"　（A=可发布 / B=修复后发布 / C=需修复 / D=不建议 / F=禁止）")
    L.append(f"- **准入卡点**：{r.get('admission_gate', '—')}"
             f"（BLOCK=禁止准入 / NEED_REVIEW=需人工复核 / REVIEW=建议关注 / PASS=可准入）")
    L.append(f"- **能力画像**：{'、'.join(r.get('capability_profile', [])) if r.get('capability_profile') else '（未检出敏感能力）'}"
             f"　（供最小权限配置参考）")
    L.append(f"- **信任认证**：{r.get('attestation', '—')}"
             f"　（借鉴 arc-security-audit：通过全部高危检查即视为可信基线）")
    L.append(f"- **策略档位**：{r.get('policy_active', 'standard')}　"
             f"**规则包**：{len(r.get('rule_pack', {}).get('applied', []))} 个规则文件生效"
             + (f"（{len(r.get('rule_pack', {}).get('skipped', []))} 个回退内置默认）"
                if r.get('rule_pack', {}).get('skipped') else ""))
    rep = r.get("reputation", {})
    if rep:
        L.append(f"- **来源信誉（v2.5.3）**：{rep.get('score', '?')}/100（{rep.get('band', '?')}）"
                 + (f"　原因：{'、'.join(rep.get('reasons', []))}" if rep.get("reasons") else ""))
    ns = r.get("nosec", {})
    if ns.get("applied"):
        L.append(f"- **误报抑制（.nosec.yml）**：已应用全局排除，抑制 {len(ns.get('suppressed', []))} 条命中"
                 "（被抑制项仍可在 --json 结果的 nosec.suppressed 字段查看，不降低透明度）。")
    if r.get("review_queue"):
        L.append(f"- **语义复核队列（v2.5.3）**：{len(r['review_queue'])} 条 P0/P1 命中需人工/Agent"
                 "复核意图（仅摘要，不含原文）——对每条判断「能力 vs 滥用」后再做准入决定。")
    if r.get("osv_findings"):
        L.append("- **OSV 依赖漏洞（--osv）**：")
        for h in r["osv_findings"]:
            L.append(f"  - **[{h['sev']}] {h['label']}**：{h['why']}")
            L.append(f"        ↳ 修复建议：{_remediation_for(h)}")
    for w in r.get("warnings", []):
        L.append(f"- {w}")
    # v3.2.0：高危 Skill 的综合研判依据（说明文件描述/形态/风险点/结论，作为风险判定依据）
    if r.get("severity_tally", {}).get("P0") or r.get("severity_tally", {}).get("P1"):
        L.append("\n" + render_verdict_rationale(r))
    L.append("\n**给使用方/审查者的防范措施：**")
    for i, m in enumerate(PREVENTION_MEASURES, 1):
        L.append(f"{i}. {m}")
    L.append("\n**参考的真实案例（来自公开安全研究）：**")
    for c in REFERENCE_CASES:
        L.append(f"- {c}")

    return "\n".join(L)


# 开关选项（无值）
_SWITCH_FLAGS = ("--json", "--dir", "--osv", "--dynamic")
# 带值选项：其后的参数是选项值，不计入位置参数（修复：选项置于路径之前时路径解析错误）
_VALUE_FLAGS = {"--sarif", "--sbom", "--policy", "--trust", "--rules-dir", "--exit-on"}
_HELP_FLAGS = ("-h", "--help")


def parse_args(args: list):
    """解析命令行参数：正确处理带值选项与开关选项。

    返回 (opts, positional)。opts 键：json/dir/osv/sarif/sbom/policy/trust/rules_dir/exit_on/help/unknown。
    - `-h/--help` 出现在任意位置都置 help=True；
    - 未知的 `--xxx` 收集到 opts["unknown"]（main 中警告并跳过，避免被误当作目标路径）；
    - 带值选项后若紧跟另一选项（如 `--policy --osv`），视为缺值（值为 None）。
    """
    opts = {"json": False, "dir": False, "osv": False,
            "dynamic": False,
            "sarif": None, "sbom": None, "policy": None, "trust": None,
            "rules_dir": None, "exit_on": None, "help": False, "unknown": []}
    positional = []
    i = 0
    while i < len(args):
        a = args[i]
        if a in _HELP_FLAGS:
            opts["help"] = True
        elif a in _SWITCH_FLAGS:
            opts[a[2:].replace("-", "_")] = True
        elif a in _VALUE_FLAGS:
            key = a[2:].replace("-", "_")
            nxt = args[i + 1] if i + 1 < len(args) else None
            if nxt is not None and not nxt.startswith("--"):
                opts[key] = nxt
                i += 1
        elif a.startswith("--"):
            opts["unknown"].append(a)
        else:
            positional.append(a)
        i += 1
    return opts, positional


HELP_TEXT = """用法: python analyze_skill.py <skill目录或SKILL.md路径> [选项]
  <path>  单个 Skill 目录/SKILL.md，或包含多个 Skill 的目录
  --json           以 JSON 输出（便于二次处理；与 --dir 联用输出结果数组）
  --dir            批量模式：把 <path> 当作含多个 Skill 的目录，输出汇总表
  --sarif <file>   导出 SARIF 2.1.0（批量模式合并为一份；CI 门禁 / 漏洞平台消费）
  --sbom <file>    导出 SBOM（CycloneDX 1.5 风格，文件级 SHA-256；批量模式合并为一份）
  --osv            开启 OSV 依赖漏洞查询（联网，默认关闭）
  --policy <file>  指定策略配置（默认 config/policy.json）
  --trust <file>   指定信任注册表（默认 config/trust.json）
  --rules-dir <dir> 指定规则包目录（默认 rules/；环境变量 SKAUDIT_RULES_DIR 亦可）
  --exit-on <sev>  命中 ≥ 该严重度时退出码为 2（默认 P1，供 CI 使用；批量模式同样生效）
  --dynamic        启用内置安全沙箱动态行为检测（仅单 Skill 模式）：隔离子进程 +
                   sys.addaudithook 审计钩子执行目标 .py，取证写上下文文件/外连/
                   执行命令/读凭据 env 等运行时行为，并阻断破坏（超时强杀）"""


def _norm_msys_path(p):
    """把 Git-Bash/MSYS 风格路径（/c/Users/...）转换为 Windows 路径（C:/Users/...）。

    在 Windows 上，裸 Path('/c/...').resolve() 会被解释为 C:\\c\\...，导致路径不存在。
    仅在 Windows 平台做此转换；Unix 上原样返回。
    """
    if not p or os.sep != "\\":
        return p
    s = os.path.expanduser(p)
    m = re.match(r"^/([a-zA-Z])(/.*)$", s)
    if m:
        s = m.group(1).upper() + ":" + m.group(2)
    return s


# ============================================================
# v3.2.4 ① 自动更新已完全移除：不再提供 --check-update / --update，
#   不再读写 config/.last_update_check 与 config/update_audit.jsonl，
#   不调用 skillhub CLI 下载/替换代码。版本更新改由用户手动从 SkillHub 拉取安装，
#   彻底消除「自动下载并执行代码」的执行链（即使被恶意 Skill 诱导也无法自更新）。
# ============================================================

# ============================================================
# v3.1.0 ② 内置安全沙箱动态行为检测（--dynamic）——纵深防御加固
# ============================================================
# 隔离子进程模板：sys.addaudithook 审计钩子取证 + 阻断破坏。3.1.0 加固点：
#   · 封 ctypes 直接调 libc 逃逸（绕过 Python 层审计钩子的最常见向量）；
#   · 封 socket.bind/listen（反弹/监听 shell）；
#   · 写隔离区外 = 沙箱逃逸（路径越界阻断 + 标记）；
#   · 被审计源码经 base64 嵌入 __SRC_B64__（磁盘不落明文）；
#   · USERPROFILE/HOME/TEMP 由父进程重定向到隔离目录；钩子内不做触发审计的动作。
#
# ⚠️ 诚实边界：进程内 Python 沙箱不是硬安全边界——攻击者可沿对象图
# （__class__.__bases__ → object.__subclasses__()）找回 os/subprocess/ctypes。
# 本沙箱定位为「运行时行为取证 + 纵深防御」：拦截常见危险操作并取证，而非保证
# 安全执行。真正硬隔离需 OS 级（容器/VM/nsjail），本环境（Windows 为主）不可用，
# 见 references/limitations.md。
_DYN_PROBE = r'''
import sys, base64, json as _json, os as _os, types as _types, re as _re, io as _io
# v3.2.8：禁止隔离子进程写 .pyc——否则「首次 import 某模块」会触发解释器向
# <python>/Lib/**/__pycache__ 写字节码，被审计钩子判为「写隔离区外=沙箱逃逸」而阻断，
# 既污染取证（伪 escape_write/delete/error），又会静默打断后续执行（b10 误报根因）。
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
_src = base64.b64decode("__SRC_B64__").decode("utf-8", "replace")
_pool = _json.loads(base64.b64decode("__POOL_B64__").decode("utf-8", "replace"))  # 其它文件长字符串池
_imp_roots = set(_json.loads(base64.b64decode("__IMP_B64__").decode("utf-8", "replace")))  # 源码显式 import 的模块根名
_events = []
_wd = _os.getcwd()  # 隔离工作目录（父进程已重定向 USERPROFILE/HOME/TEMP 至此）
_BLOCK_IMPORTS = ("ctypes", "ctypes.util", "ctypes.wintypes")
# v3.2.8 对象图逃逸运行时加固（对应沙箱边界 #5301 的诚实声明）：
#   _GRAPH_BLOCK：终端危险属性——读到即等同于在找回 os/subprocess/ctypes，阻断并取证；
#   _GRAPH_RECON：侦察类属性——仅取证（P2），避免正当元编程被误伤；
#   _TRIP_CALLS：C 层调用 tripwire（sys.setprofile 的 c_call 事件可捕获直接属性链调用，
#                如 x.__class__.__bases__[0].__subclasses__()，这是 Python 审计钩子盲区——
#                CPython 没有 object.__getattr__ 审计事件，直接属性访问无法被钩子观测）。
_GRAPH_BLOCK = ("__subclasses__", "__globals__", "__builtins__",
                "f_globals", "f_back", "f_locals", "gi_frame", "cr_frame")
_GRAPH_RECON = ("__bases__", "__base__", "__mro__", "__code__", "__closure__",
                "__getattribute__", "gi_code", "__subclasshook__")
_TRIP_CALLS = ("__subclasses__", "load_module", "find_class")
_DANGER_MOD_ROOTS = ("os", "subprocess", "ctypes", "socket", "posix", "nt", "_socket")
# 敏感凭据路径（读取即取证）：沙箱内本不该存在这些文件，命中说明脚本在采集凭据。
_SENS_PATH_RE = _re.compile(
    r"(\.ssh[/\\]|id_rsa|id_ed25519|\.aws[/\\]credentials|\.npmrc|\.pypirc|\.netrc|"
    r"\.docker[/\\]config|credentials\.json|service[_-]?account|\.kube[/\\]config|"
    r"\.env\b|secrets?\.(ya?ml|json|txt)|\.gnupg[/\\]|token\.json|\.git-credentials)",
    _re.I)
# v3.2.8 白名单 HTTP 客户端取证 stub：隔离子进程以 -I 启动（无站点包），requests/httpx
# 等第三方库 ImportError 使外传行为在沙箱内不可复现（m13/m18 长期判 not_applicable）。
# 此处仅当真实库不可用时注入同名轻量 stub，使 post/put/send 等调用触发 net_http 取证。
_HTTP_STUBS = {
    "requests": ("get", "post", "put", "patch", "delete", "head", "options", "request"),
    "httpx": ("get", "post", "put", "patch", "delete", "head", "options", "request", "stream"),
    "urllib3": ("request",),
    "aiohttp": ("request",),
    "curl_cffi": ("get", "post", "put", "request"),
    "websocket": ("send", "send_text", "send_binary", "create_connection"),
    "websockets": ("send", "connect"),
}
def _in_workdir(p):
    try:
        rp = _os.path.realpath(p)
        return rp == _wd or rp.startswith(_wd + _os.sep)
    except Exception:
        return False
def _hook(event, args):
    try:
        rec = None
        if event == "import":
            # 3.1.0：拦截 ctypes 直接调 libc（绕过 Python 层审计钩子的最常见逃逸向量）
            name = args[0] if args else ""
            if isinstance(name, str) and name.split(".")[0] in _BLOCK_IMPORTS:
                rec = ("import_block", name)
        elif event == "exec":
            # 3.2.8：跨文件拼接执行取证（GhostSplice 分片重组，DEF CON 34）
            # exec/eval 运行时 code 对象的字符串常量若与"其它文件"的载荷片段互为子串，
            # 说明当前脚本把来自别处的分片组装成完整攻击后执行。
            cb = args[0] if args else None
            cts = []
            try:
                cts = [c for c in cb.co_consts if isinstance(c, str) and len(c) >= 12]
            except Exception:
                cts = []
            for c in cts:
                for q in _pool:
                    if len(q) >= 16 and (c in q or q in c):
                        rec = ("splice_exec", q[:80])
                        break
                if rec:
                    break
        elif event == "open":
            p, m = (args[0] if len(args) > 0 else ""), (args[1] if len(args) > 1 else "")
            # 解释器自身的字节码缓存写入属运行时噪声（且 dont_write_bytecode 已关闭该行为），
            # 不能当作「脚本写隔离区外」。
            if isinstance(p, str) and ("__pycache__" in p or p.endswith(".pyc")):
                pass
            elif isinstance(p, str) and isinstance(m, str) and any(c in m for c in "wa+"):
                rec = ("escape_write", p) if not _in_workdir(p) else ("write", p)
            elif isinstance(p, str) and _SENS_PATH_RE.search(p.replace("\\", "/")) and not _in_workdir(p):
                rec = ("read_secret", p)
        elif event in ("subprocess.Popen", "os.system", "os.spawn", "posix.spawn", "os.exec"):
            rec = ("exec", str(args[0]) if args else "")
        elif event == "socket.connect":
            rec = ("net", str(args[0]) if args else "")
        elif event in ("socket.bind", "socket.listen"):
            rec = ("bind", str(args[0]) if args else "")  # 3.1.0：反弹/监听 shell
        elif event in ("os.remove", "os.unlink", "os.rename", "os.rmdir", "os.removedirs", "shutil.rmtree"):
            rec = ("delete", str(args[0]) if args else "")
        elif event in ("shutil.copyfile", "shutil.copy2", "shutil.copytree", "os.replace"):
            # copyfile/copy2 在 C 加速实现（_shutil）下不触发 "open" 审计，需独立捕获
            tgt = str(args[1]) if len(args) > 1 else (str(args[0]) if args else "")
            rec = ("escape_write", tgt) if not _in_workdir(tgt) else ("write", tgt)
        elif event == "os.environ":
            rec = ("env", str(args[0]) if args else "")
        if rec:
            _events.append(rec)
            if rec[0] in ("exec", "net", "delete", "bind", "import_block", "escape_write"):
                raise ValueError("blocked-by-audit:" + rec[0])
    except ValueError:
        raise
    except Exception:
        pass
sys.addaudithook(_hook)


# ---------- v3.2.8 ① HTTP 客户端取证 stub ----------
class _StubResp(object):
    status_code, reason, text, url = 200, "OK", "", ""
    content, headers = b"", {}

    def json(self, *a, **k):
        return {}

    def raise_for_status(self, *a, **k):
        return None

    def iter_content(self, *a, **k):
        return iter(())

    def iter_lines(self, *a, **k):
        return iter(())

    def close(self):
        return None

    def __enter__(self):
        return self

    def __exit__(self, *a):
        return False


def _rec_net(mod, verb, url):
    try:
        _events.append(("net_http", mod + "." + verb + " " + str(url)[:120]))
    except Exception:
        pass


def _mk_stub(modname, verbs):
    m = _types.ModuleType(modname)

    def _mk(verb):
        def _f(url=None, *a, **k):
            _rec_net(modname, verb, url)
            return _StubResp()
        return _f

    class _StubSession(object):
        def __init__(self, *a, **k):
            pass

        def __enter__(self):
            return self

        def __exit__(self, *a):
            return False

        def close(self):
            return None

    def _mk_sess(verb):
        def _f(self, url=None, *a, **k):
            _rec_net(modname, "Session." + verb, url)
            return _StubResp()
        return _f

    for v in verbs:
        setattr(m, v, _mk(v))
        setattr(_StubSession, v, _mk_sess(v))
    m.Session = _StubSession
    m.ClientSession = _StubSession
    m.AsyncClient = _StubSession
    m.Response = _StubResp
    return m


for _mn, _verbs in _HTTP_STUBS.items():
    if _mn in sys.modules:
        continue
    try:
        __import__(_mn)  # 真实库可用时不覆盖（保持真实行为与真实审计事件）
    except Exception:
        sys.modules[_mn] = _mk_stub(_mn, _verbs)


# ---------- v3.2.8 ② 对象图逃逸：受控 builtins（getattr 链取证/阻断） ----------
_bd = dict(vars(__builtins__)) if not isinstance(__builtins__, dict) else dict(__builtins__)
_orig_getattr = _bd.get("getattr", getattr)


def _guarded_getattr(obj, name, *default):
    if isinstance(name, str):
        if name in _GRAPH_BLOCK:
            _events.append(("escape_graph", "getattr(%s,%s)" % (type(obj).__name__, name)))
            if not default:  # 三参数形式（带回退值）多为正当元编程，仅取证不阻断
                raise ValueError("blocked-by-audit:escape_graph")
        elif name in _GRAPH_RECON:
            _events.append(("escape_probe", "getattr(%s,%s)" % (type(obj).__name__, name)))
    return _orig_getattr(obj, name, *default)


_bd["getattr"] = _guarded_getattr

# v3.2.8 ②b 受控 open：沙箱内不存在真实凭据文件，若脚本读取敏感凭据路径，
# 合成占位内容使「读凭据 → 编码 → 外传」整条窃密链可在沙箱中完整复现（否则读文件即
# FileNotFoundError，外传调用永远不会执行，取证停在半途）。仅对「不存在 + 敏感路径」生效。
_orig_open = _bd.get("open", open)


def _guarded_open(file, mode="r", *a, **k):
    try:
        p = str(file)
        m = mode if isinstance(mode, str) else "r"
        if (not any(c in m for c in "wa+")) and not _in_workdir(p) \
                and _SENS_PATH_RE.search(p.replace("\\", "/")) and not _os.path.exists(p):
            _events.append(("read_secret", p))
            return (_io.BytesIO(b"sandbox-synthesized-placeholder") if "b" in m
                    else _io.StringIO("sandbox-synthesized-placeholder"))
    except Exception:
        pass
    return _orig_open(file, mode, *a, **k)


_bd["open"] = _guarded_open


# ---------- v3.2.8 ③ 对象图逃逸：C 调用 tripwire（直接属性链盲区） ----------
_prof_busy = [False]
_prof_n = [0]


def _prof(frame, event, arg):
    if _prof_busy[0]:
        return None
    _prof_busy[0] = True
    try:
        if event == "c_call":
            nm = getattr(arg, "__name__", "")
            if nm in _TRIP_CALLS:
                _events.append(("escape_graph", "call:" + nm))
                _prof_n[0] += 1
                if _prof_n[0] >= 12:  # 取证足够即关闭，避免大循环被 profile 拖垮
                    sys.setprofile(None)
    except Exception:
        pass
    _prof_busy[0] = False
    return None


def _scan_recovered(ns):
    """执行后命名空间扫描：沿对象图找回并赋给变量的 os/subprocess/ctypes 等模块，
    若未出现在源码 import 列表中 → 运行时实证的沙箱逃逸。"""
    try:
        for k, v in list(ns.items()):
            if k.startswith("__") or not isinstance(v, _types.ModuleType):
                continue
            mn = getattr(v, "__name__", "") or ""
            root = mn.split(".")[0]
            if root in _DANGER_MOD_ROOTS and root not in _imp_roots:
                _events.append(("escape_graph", "recovered-module:" + mn))
    except Exception:
        pass


try:
    _c = compile(_src, "<dyn>", "exec")
except SyntaxError:
    _events.append(("error", "SyntaxError"))
    _c = None
_g = {"__name__": "__dyn__", "__file__": "<dyn>", "__builtins__": _bd}
if _c is not None:
    try:
        sys.setprofile(_prof)
    except Exception:
        pass
    try:
        exec(_c, _g)
    except SystemExit:
        pass
    except BaseException as e:
        _events.append(("error", type(e).__name__))
    finally:
        try:
            sys.setprofile(None)
        except Exception:
            pass
        _scan_recovered(_g)
print("DYN_EVENTS " + _json.dumps(_events, ensure_ascii=False))
'''

_SENS_CTX_RE = re.compile(r"(SOUL\.md|AGENTS\.md|HEARTBEAT\.md|CLAUDE\.md|MEMORY\.md|\.clawdbot)", re.I)
_SKILLS_DIR_RE = re.compile(r"[\\/]skills[\\/]", re.I)
_CRED_ENV_RE = re.compile(r"(API[_-]?KEY|TOKEN|SECRET|PASSWORD|PASSWD|CREDENTIAL)", re.I)


# 3.1.0：AST 预筛——隔离子进程执行前对目标源码做语法树扫描，识别沙箱逃逸基元。
# 与运行时审计钩子互补：钩子拦截"操作"，AST 拦截"尝试逃逸的代码结构"
# （对象图遍历找回 os/subprocess、ctypes/marshal 直接调用、getattr/__import__ 字符串构造）。
_ESCAPE_ATTRS = ("__subclasses__", "__globals__", "__builtins__", "__class__",
                 "__bases__", "__mro__", "__subclasshook__", "__code__", "__closure__",
                 "__getattribute__", "__dict__",
                 # 3.2.1：栈帧逃逸基元（生成器/协程帧回溯绕过审计钩子，2026 mossfern/GFCTF 实证）
                 "gi_frame", "f_back", "f_globals", "f_locals", "f_code",
                 "cr_frame", "gi_code", "gi_yieldfrom")
_ESCAPE_NAMES = ("ctypes", "marshal", "importlib", "__import__", "pickle", "_pickle")
_ESCAPE_CALLS = ("getattr", "__import__", "exec", "eval", "compile")


def _ast_imported_roots(src: str) -> set:
    """从源码提取显式 import 的模块根名（用于区分「正常导入」与「沿对象图找回的模块」）。"""
    roots = set()
    try:
        tree = ast.parse(src, "<dyn>")
    except SyntaxError:
        return roots
    for node in ast.walk(tree):
        if isinstance(node, ast.Import):
            for al in node.names:
                roots.add((al.name or "").split(".")[0])
        elif isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom) and node.level == 0 and node.module:
            roots.add(node.module.split(".")[0])
    return roots


def _attr_chain(node) -> str:
    """还原属性链源码片段（a.b.c → 'a.b.c'），用于判定多级对象图遍历。"""
    parts = []
    cur = node
    while isinstance(cur, ast.Attribute):
        parts.append(cur.attr)
        cur = cur.value
    if isinstance(cur, ast.Name):
        parts.append(cur.id)
    elif isinstance(cur, ast.Constant):
        parts.append(repr(cur.value))
    elif isinstance(cur, ast.Call):
        parts.append("()")
    return ".".join(reversed(parts))


def _dynamic_ast_screen(src: str) -> list:
    """对目标源码做 AST 沙箱逃逸基元预筛，返回 (pattern, snippet) 列表（仅标记，不阻断）。"""
    hits = []
    try:
        tree = ast.parse(src, "<dyn>")
    except SyntaxError:
        return hits
    for node in ast.walk(tree):
        if isinstance(node, ast.Attribute) and node.attr in _ESCAPE_ATTRS:
            seg = ""
            try:
                seg = (ast.get_source_segment(src, node) or "")[:90]
            except Exception:
                pass
            # 3.2.8：多级链（a.__class__.__bases__[0].__subclasses__）比单词属性置信度高得多
            chain = _attr_chain(node)
            if chain.count(".") >= 2 and sum(1 for p in chain.split(".") if p in _ESCAPE_ATTRS) >= 2:
                hits.append(("ast_escape_chain", chain[:120]))
            else:
                hits.append(("ast_escape_attr", f"{node.attr}:{seg}"))
        elif (isinstance(node, ast.Call) and isinstance(node.func, ast.Name)
              and node.func.id == "getattr" and len(node.args) >= 2
              and isinstance(node.args[1], ast.Constant)
              and isinstance(node.args[1].value, str)
              and node.args[1].value in _ESCAPE_ATTRS):
            # 3.2.8：字符串化 getattr 链（getattr(getattr(x,'__class__'),'__base__')）——
            # 规避「属性名出现在源码」的静态特征，必须与直接属性链同等对待（P1 而非 P2）。
            hits.append(("ast_escape_attr",
                         f"getattr:{node.args[1].value}:{(ast.get_source_segment(src, node) or '')[:80]}"))
        elif isinstance(node, ast.Name) and node.id in _ESCAPE_NAMES:
            hits.append(("ast_escape_name", node.id))
        elif isinstance(node, ast.Call) and isinstance(node.func, ast.Name) and node.func.id in _ESCAPE_CALLS:
            hits.append(("ast_escape_call", node.func.id))
        elif (isinstance(node, ast.Call) and isinstance(node.func, ast.Name)
              and node.func.id in ("exec", "eval", "compile")
              and node.args
              and isinstance(node.args[0], ast.BinOp)
              and isinstance(node.args[0].op, ast.Add)):
            # 3.2.8：拼接后动态执行（GhostSplice 分片重组静态预筛）——eval/exec/compile 首参为
            # 字符串相加表达式（常量折叠或变量拼接），是跨文件分片组装后执行的源码级信号。
            hits.append(("ast_splice", node.func.id))
    return hits


def _dynamic_run_one(py_path: Path, workdir: Path, timeout: int = 5, pool: list = None):
    """对单个 .py 文件做动态沙箱执行（隔离子进程，stdin 传码），返回事件列表（含 AST 预筛标记）。

    pool=同 Skill 其它 .py 文件的长字符串常量（GhostSplice 拼接取证用，默认空）。
    """
    try:
        src = py_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    except OSError:
        return [("error", "read-fail")]
    if not src.strip():
        return []
    if len(src) > 512 * 1024:
        return [("error", "too-large")]
    ast_hits = _dynamic_ast_screen(src)  # 3.1.0：执行前 AST 逃逸基元预筛
    b64 = base64.b64encode(src.encode("utf-8")).decode("ascii")
    pool_b64 = base64.b64encode(json.dumps(list(pool or [])).encode("utf-8")).decode("ascii")
    imp_b64 = base64.b64encode(json.dumps(sorted(_ast_imported_roots(src))).encode("utf-8")).decode("ascii")
    probe = (_DYN_PROBE.replace("__SRC_B64__", b64)
             .replace("__POOL_B64__", pool_b64)
             .replace("__IMP_B64__", imp_b64))
    env = {"PATH": os.environ.get("PATH", ""),
           "SystemRoot": os.environ.get("SystemRoot", ""),
           "TEMP": str(workdir), "TMP": str(workdir),
           "USERPROFILE": str(workdir), "HOME": str(workdir)}
    try:
        proc = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, "-I", "-S", "-c", probe],
                                cwd=str(workdir), env=env,
                                stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE)
    except Exception as e:
        return ast_hits + [("error", type(e).__name__)]
    try:
        out, _err = proc.communicate(timeout=timeout)
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        try:
            subprocess.run(["taskkill", "/F", "/T", "/PID", str(proc.pid)],
                           capture_output=True, timeout=15)
        except Exception:
            try:
                proc.kill()
            except Exception:
                pass
        return ast_hits + [("timeout", "")]
    text = out.decode("utf-8", "replace")
    for line in text.splitlines():
        if line.startswith("DYN_EVENTS "):
            try:
                return ast_hits + json.loads(line[len("DYN_EVENTS "):])
            except Exception:
                return ast_hits + [("error", "parse-fail")]
    return ast_hits + [("error", "no-events")]


# ================================================================ v3.3.2 新增（P0-2）
# 沙箱隔离后端探测与降级层：探测 Docker / Windows Sandbox；可用则走真隔离，
# 不可用自动降级为进程内审计钩子并**显式标注隔离级别**（不静默降级）。
_DYNAMIC_ISOLATION = {"backend": "unspecified", "hard": False, "note": ""}


def detect_isolation_backends():
    """探测可用的动态取证隔离后端（不执行任何被测代码）。"""
    info = {"docker": False, "winsandbox": False, "inprocess": True, "detail": {}}
    try:
        d = shutil.which("docker")
        info["docker"] = bool(d)
        info["detail"]["docker"] = d or "not found"
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        info["detail"]["docker"] = f"probe error: {exc}"
    try:
        if os.name == "nt":
            wd = os.environ.get("WINDIR", r"C:\Windows")
            p = os.path.join(wd, "System32", "WindowsSandbox.exe")
            info["winsandbox"] = os.path.exists(p)
            info["detail"]["winsandbox"] = p if info["winsandbox"] else "not found"
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        info["detail"]["winsandbox"] = f"probe error: {exc}"
    return info


def select_isolation():
    """选择隔离后端：Docker > Windows Sandbox > 进程内审计钩子（降级）。"""
    b = detect_isolation_backends()
    if b["docker"]:
        return {"backend": "docker(container)", "hard": True,
                "note": "Docker 容器隔离：--network=none 断网 + 只读挂载 + cap-drop ALL + 资源限额。",
                "probe": b}
    if b["winsandbox"]:
        return {"backend": "windows-sandbox", "hard": True,
                "note": "Windows Sandbox：断网 + 只读映射；GUI 型环境，自动行为采集能力有限。",
                "probe": b}
    return {"backend": "in-process-audithook", "hard": False,
            "note": "降级：无 Docker / Windows Sandbox，使用进程内审计钩子 + 隔离 workdir。"
                    "该模式**非硬安全边界**，仅作运行时取证与纵深防御；硬隔离请在 OS 级容器/VM 中运行。",
            "probe": b}


def _docker_run_one(rel: str, workdir: Path, timeout: int = 5):
    """Docker 后端：在真隔离容器内执行单个脚本并采集输出（best-effort）。"""
    img = os.environ.get("SKAUDIT_DOCKER_IMAGE", "python:3.12-alpine")
    cmd = ["docker", "run", "--rm", "--network=none", "--read-only",
           "--cap-drop", "ALL", "--memory", "512m", "--pids-limit", "128",
           "-v", f"{workdir}:/work:ro", "-w", "/work", img, "python", rel]
    try:
        proc = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout + 30)
        out = ((proc.stdout or "") + (proc.stderr or ""))[:2000]
        return [["docker_exit", str(proc.returncode)], ["docker_output", out]]
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        # 容器不可用/超时 → 交由调用方回退到进程内后端
        return [["error", "DockerBackendError", str(exc)[:200]]]


def dynamic_probe(skill_dir: Path, max_files: int = 8, timeout: int = 5):
    """对 Skill 目录下 .py 脚本做安全动态沙箱执行，返回 {相对路径: 事件列表}。

    v3.3.2：先探测隔离后端（Docker / Windows Sandbox / 降级），并写入模块级
    `_DYNAMIC_ISOLATION` 供报告与告警使用；Docker 后端失败时自动回退进程内后端。
    """
    global _DYNAMIC_ISOLATION
    _DYNAMIC_ISOLATION = select_isolation()
    result = {}
    if not skill_dir.is_dir():
        return result
    try:
        tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="skdyn-")
    except OSError:
        return result
    with tmp:
        work = Path(tmp.name)
        # 复制文本类文件到隔离目录：让「读取自身/他文件分片再拼接执行」（GhostSplice）、
        # 自传播样本的真实执行流可在沙箱复现；隔离区内被覆盖/改写无实际危害（写越界由钩子阻断）。
        _TXT_EXT = {".py", ".md", ".txt", ".json", ".yml", ".yaml", ".toml",
                    ".ini", ".cfg", ".conf", ".sh", ".bat", ".ps1", ".js", ".ts"}
        for p in sorted(skill_dir.rglob("*")):
            if not p.is_file() or "__pycache__" in str(p) or is_self_excluded(p):
                continue
            if p.suffix.lower() not in _TXT_EXT:
                continue
            try:
                if p.stat().st_size > 1024 * 1024:
                    continue
                rel = p.relative_to(skill_dir)
                dst = work / rel
                dst.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                dst.write_bytes(p.read_bytes())
            except OSError:
                pass
        n = 0
        # 先收集全部 .py 的「长字符串常量池」，供跨文件拼接取证（GhostSplice）。
        str_map = {}
        for p in sorted(skill_dir.rglob("*.py")):
            rel = str(p.relative_to(skill_dir)).replace("\\", "/")
            if "__pycache__" in rel or is_self_excluded(p):
                continue
            try:
                tree = ast.parse(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"))
            except Exception:
                continue
            consts = []
            for nd in ast.walk(tree):
                if isinstance(nd, ast.Constant) and isinstance(nd.value, str) and len(nd.value) >= 16:
                    consts.append(nd.value)
            str_map[rel] = consts[:400]
        for p in sorted(skill_dir.rglob("*.py")):
            rel = str(p.relative_to(skill_dir)).replace("\\", "/")
            if "__pycache__" in rel or is_self_excluded(p):
                continue
            n += 1
            if n > max_files:
                break
            excl = set(str_map.get(rel, ()))
            pool = [s for lst in str_map.values()
                    for s in lst if s not in excl][:800]
            if _DYNAMIC_ISOLATION.get("hard") and _DYNAMIC_ISOLATION.get("backend", "").startswith("docker"):
                ev = _docker_run_one(rel, work, timeout)
                if ev and ev[0][:1] == ["error"]:
                    # 容器不可用 → 回退进程内后端（诚实降级）
                    _DYNAMIC_ISOLATION["backend"] = "in-process-audithook (docker 回退)"
                    _DYNAMIC_ISOLATION["hard"] = False
                    _DYNAMIC_ISOLATION["note"] = ("Docker 后端不可用，已回退进程内审计钩子（非硬安全边界）。"
                                                  f"原因：{ev[0][-1] if len(ev[0]) > 2 else 'unknown'}")
                    ev = _dynamic_run_one(p, work, timeout, pool)
                result[rel] = _norm_sandbox_paths(ev, work)
            else:
                result[rel] = _norm_sandbox_paths(_dynamic_run_one(p, work, timeout, pool), work)
    return result


def scan_dynamic_behavior(dyn_map: dict) -> list:
    """把动态沙箱事件映射为严重度命中（与静态结果互补的运行时实证）。"""
    hits = []
    if not dyn_map:
        return hits
    for rel, events in dyn_map.items():
        for ev in events:
            if not isinstance(ev, (list, tuple)) or not ev:
                continue
            kind, detail = ev[0], (ev[1] if len(ev) > 1 else "")
            if kind == "timeout":
                hits.append({"sev": "P3", "label": "动态执行超时（疑似死循环/自保）",
                             "why": f"{rel} 在隔离沙箱中运行超过 {5} 秒被强杀，可能含死循环、"
                                    f"反分析或恶意休眠逻辑，建议人工复核。",
                             "snippet": f"{rel}: timeout", "line": 0})
            elif kind == "write":
                if _SENS_CTX_RE.search(detail):
                    hits.append({"sev": "P1", "label": "动态实证：运行时写入 Agent 上下文/凭据文件",
                                 "why": f"{rel} 运行时尝试写入 {detail}（隔离区已重定向，未落真实磁盘），"
                                        f"与 ClawHavoc「认知根工具」持久化手法一致，静态扫描往往不可见。",
                                 "snippet": f"{rel}: write {detail}", "line": 0})
                elif _SKILLS_DIR_RE.search(detail):
                    hits.append({"sev": "P1", "label": "动态实证：向技能目录写入（自传播）",
                                 "why": f"{rel} 运行时尝试写入 {detail}，语义蠕虫自传播特征。",
                                 "snippet": f"{rel}: write {detail}", "line": 0})
                else:
                    hits.append({"sev": "P2", "label": "动态实证：运行时写入文件",
                                 "why": f"{rel} 运行时写入 {detail}（隔离区内，未落真实磁盘）。",
                                 "snippet": f"{rel}: write {detail[:100]}", "line": 0})
            elif kind == "exec":
                hits.append({"sev": "P1", "label": "动态实证：执行外部命令（已阻断）",
                             "why": f"{rel} 运行时调用系统命令（沙箱审计钩子已阻断）：{detail[:160]}。",
                             "snippet": f"{rel}: exec {detail[:80]}", "line": 0})
            elif kind == "splice_exec":
                hits.append({"sev": "P1", "label": "动态实证：跨文件拼接后执行（GhostSplice 分片重组）",
                             "why": (f"{rel} 运行时 eval/exec 的载荷常量与其它文件的载荷片段互为子串，"
                                     f"即把分散于不同文件的分片组装成完整命令后执行（GhostSplice，"
                                     f"DEF CON 34）。片段：{detail[:80]}…"),
                             "snippet": f"{rel}: splice {detail[:60]}", "line": 0})
            elif kind == "net":
                hits.append({"sev": "P1", "label": "动态实证：发起网络连接（已阻断）",
                             "why": f"{rel} 运行时尝试网络连接（已阻断）：{detail[:160]}。",
                             "snippet": f"{rel}: net {detail[:80]}", "line": 0})
            elif kind == "net_http":
                # v3.2.8：第三方 HTTP 客户端（requests/httpx/…）在 -I 隔离下无站点包，
                # 沙箱注入白名单取证 stub 使外传可复现。写类动词 = 外传（P1）；
                # 读类动词（GET/HEAD/OPTIONS）仅提示（P2），避免把正常 API 调用判高危。
                _is_write = bool(re.search(
                    r"\.(post|put|patch|send|send_text|send_binary|create_connection|stream)\b",
                    detail, re.I))
                if _is_write:
                    hits.append({"sev": "P1", "label": "动态实证：HTTP 客户端外传（沙箱 stub 取证，已阻断）",
                                 "why": (f"{rel} 运行时经 HTTP 客户端发起外传请求（已阻断）：{detail[:160]}。"
                                         f"隔离子进程无第三方站点包，该调用由白名单取证 stub 捕获；"
                                         f"与凭据读取/环境采集组合即构成典型窃密链。"),
                                 "snippet": f"{rel}: net_http {detail[:80]}", "line": 0})
                else:
                    hits.append({"sev": "P2", "label": "动态实证：HTTP 出站请求（沙箱 stub 取证）",
                                 "why": (f"{rel} 运行时经 HTTP 客户端发起出站请求（读类动词）：{detail[:160]}。"
                                         f"属正常 API 调用常见形态，仅提示其存在网络出站。"),
                                 "snippet": f"{rel}: net_http {detail[:80]}", "line": 0})
            elif kind == "read_secret":
                # v3.2.8：读敏感凭据路径（沙箱内源文件不存在，取证由受控 open 合成占位内容触发）。
                # 定 P2 而非 P1：读取凭据本身有正当场景（本工具配置加载），需与外传/命令执行组合才定性。
                hits.append({"sev": "P2", "label": "动态实证：读取敏感凭据路径（沙箱取证）",
                             "why": (f"{rel} 运行时读取敏感凭据路径 {detail[:160]}。"
                                     f"沙箱内不存在该文件（已合成占位内容以便后续链路继续复现）；"
                                     f"若与外传调用同时出现，即构成「读凭据 + 外传」窃密链。"),
                             "snippet": f"{rel}: read_secret {detail[:80]}", "line": 0})
            elif kind == "escape_graph":
                hits.append({"sev": "P0", "label": "动态实证：沙箱对象图逃逸（运行时取证/已阻断）",
                             "why": (f"{rel} 运行时沿对象图找回受限能力：{detail[:160]}。"
                                     f"进程内 Python 沙箱无法阻止对象图遍历（无 object.__getattr__ 审计事件），"
                                     f"但该行为本身即「试图突破审计边界」的强恶意信号——"
                                     f"正常 Skill 不会去还原系统模块。取证方式：受控 getattr 阻断 / "
                                     f"C 调用 tripwire / 执行后命名空间回收扫描。"),
                             "snippet": f"{rel}: escape_graph {detail[:80]}", "line": 0})
            elif kind == "escape_probe":
                hits.append({"sev": "P2", "label": "动态预筛（运行时）：读取对象图危险属性",
                             "why": (f"{rel} 运行时读取对象图危险属性 {detail[:120]}（取证不阻断）。"
                                     f"单独出现多为元编程，与命令执行/外传组合时需人工复核。"),
                             "snippet": f"{rel}: escape_probe {detail[:80]}", "line": 0})
            elif kind == "delete":
                hits.append({"sev": "P1", "label": "动态实证：删除文件（已阻断）",
                             "why": f"{rel} 运行时尝试删除 {detail}（已阻断），破坏性行为。",
                             "snippet": f"{rel}: delete {detail[:80]}", "line": 0})
            elif kind == "import_block":
                hits.append({"sev": "P1", "label": "动态实证：尝试导入受限模块（已阻断）",
                             "why": f"{rel} 运行时尝试导入 {detail}（沙箱审计钩子已拦截），"
                                    f"该模块常绕过 Python 层审计直接调系统调用，是典型逃逸向量。",
                             "snippet": f"{rel}: import {detail}", "line": 0})
            elif kind == "bind":
                hits.append({"sev": "P1", "label": "动态实证：监听/绑定端口（已阻断）",
                             "why": f"{rel} 运行时尝试 socket 监听/绑定（已阻断）：{detail[:160]}，"
                                    f"可能为反弹/监听 shell 或服务端后门。",
                             "snippet": f"{rel}: bind {detail[:80]}", "line": 0})
            elif kind == "escape_write":
                hits.append({"sev": "P0", "label": "动态实证：尝试写隔离区外（沙箱逃逸）",
                             "why": f"{rel} 运行时尝试写入隔离区外路径 {detail}（已阻断），"
                                    f"表明隔离重定向被绕过，属明确沙箱逃逸特征。",
                             "snippet": f"{rel}: escape_write {detail[:80]}", "line": 0})
            elif kind in ("ast_escape_attr", "ast_escape_name", "ast_escape_call", "ast_escape_chain"):
                if kind == "ast_escape_chain":
                    sev, label = "P1", "动态预筛：多级对象图逃逸链（高置信）"
                    why = (f"{rel} 含多级对象图遍历链 {detail}，即沿类型/基类链逐级回溯以绕过"
                           f"审计钩子找回受限模块，比单词属性命中置信度高，建议优先复核。")
                elif kind == "ast_escape_attr":
                    sev, label = "P1", "动态预筛：沙箱逃逸基元（对象图遍历/危险属性）"
                    why = (f"{rel} 含逃逸基元 {detail}，可能试图沿对象图找回 os/subprocess 等，"
                           f"属已知沙箱逃逸手法，建议人工复核。")
                elif kind == "ast_escape_name":
                    sev, label = "P2", "动态预筛：受限模块引用"
                    why = (f"{rel} 引用受限模块 {detail}（如 ctypes/marshal/importlib），"
                           f"可能用于绕过审计直接调系统调用。")
                else:
                    sev, label = "P2", "动态预筛：危险调用基元"
                    why = (f"{rel} 调用 {detail}（getattr/__import__/exec/eval/compile），"
                           f"常用于字符串构造危险名或动态执行，建议人工复核。")
                hits.append({"sev": sev, "label": label, "why": why,
                             "snippet": f"{rel}: {detail[:100]}", "line": 0})
            elif kind == "ast_splice":
                hits.append({"sev": "P1", "label": "动态预筛：拼接后动态执行（GhostSplice 分片重组）",
                             "why": (f"{rel} 对字符串相加表达式调用 {detail}（eval/exec/compile）——"
                                     f"分片指令在代码层被拼回完整载荷后执行（GhostSplice，DEF CON 34 "
                                     f"ASSET 演示；直接请求被拒、分片拼接成功的攻击路径）。"),
                             "snippet": f"{rel}: {detail}(a+b…)", "line": 0})
            elif kind == "env":
                if _CRED_ENV_RE.search(detail):
                    hits.append({"sev": "P2", "label": "动态实证：读取敏感环境变量",
                                 "why": f"{rel} 运行时读取含凭据名的环境变量 {detail}。",
                                 "snippet": f"{rel}: env {detail[:80]}", "line": 0})
            elif kind == "error":
                hits.append({"sev": "P3", "label": "动态执行异常",
                             "why": f"{rel} 在沙箱中执行异常（{detail}），可能为运行时错误或反分析退出。",
                             "snippet": f"{rel}: {detail}", "line": 0})
    return hits


def main():
    args = sys.argv[1:]
    opts, positional = parse_args(args)
    if not args or opts.get("help"):
        print(HELP_TEXT)
        sys.exit(0)
    for u in opts.get("unknown", []):
        print(f"⚠️ 忽略未知选项：{u}", file=sys.stderr)
    if not positional:
        print("错误：缺少目标路径（Skill 目录或 SKILL.md）。")
        sys.exit(1)
    as_json, as_dir, osv = opts["json"], opts["dir"], opts["osv"]
    sarif = _norm_msys_path(opts["sarif"])
    sbom = _norm_msys_path(opts["sbom"])
    policy_f = _norm_msys_path(opts["policy"])
    trust_f = _norm_msys_path(opts["trust"])
    rules_dir = _norm_msys_path(opts["rules_dir"])
    exit_on = opts["exit_on"] or "P1"
    path = Path(_norm_msys_path(positional[0])).expanduser().resolve()
    if not path.exists():
        # v3.3.3 修复（接口契约）：--json 模式下输出结构化 JSON 错误对象，
        # 供批量并行子进程 / CI 解析器消费（此前输出纯文本导致 json.loads 失败）。
        if as_json:
            print(json.dumps({
                "error": f"路径不存在：{path}",
                "skill_path": str(path),
                "admission_gate": "ERROR（无法完成扫描）",
                "verdict": "目标路径不存在，请检查路径是否正确。",
                "warnings": [f"路径不存在：{path}"],
            }, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str))
        else:
            print(f"❌ 路径不存在：{path}")
            print("【最终判定】ERROR（无法完成扫描）｜目标路径不存在，请检查路径是否正确。")
        sys.exit(1)

    # 自定义规则包 / 策略 / 信任（覆盖模块默认；信息打印一律走 stderr，避免污染 --json 输出）
    if rules_dir:
        global _RULE_PACK_STATUS
        _RULE_PACK_STATUS = {"applied": [], "skipped": []}
        _RULE_PACK_STATUS["applied"], _RULE_PACK_STATUS["skipped"] = load_rule_pack(rules_dir)
        print("规则包加载：应用 " + str(len(_RULE_PACK_STATUS["applied"])) + " 个规则文件"
              + (f"；回退内置：{len(_RULE_PACK_STATUS['skipped'])} 个" if _RULE_PACK_STATUS["skipped"] else ""),
              file=sys.stderr)
        for s in _RULE_PACK_STATUS["skipped"]:
            print("  ⚠️ ", s, file=sys.stderr)
    if policy_f or trust_f:
        global POLICY, TRUST
        if policy_f:
            POLICY = load_policy(policy_f)
        if trust_f:
            TRUST = load_trust(trust_f)

    # 自动判定：目录不含 SKILL.md 但子目录含 → 批量模式（显式 --json 时不自动切换，避免歧义）
    if path.is_dir() and not (path / "SKILL.md").exists() and not as_json:
        if any(p.name == "SKILL.md" for p in path.rglob("SKILL.md")):
            as_dir = True

    exit_code = 0
    sev_order = {"P0": 0, "P1": 1, "P2": 2, "P3": 3}
    threshold = sev_order.get((exit_on or "P1").upper(), 1)

    if as_dir:
        # v3.3.2：批量并行（进程级，保守）——仅目标数 >10 且未启用动态取证时并行；
        # --dynamic 强制串行（动态取证涉及隔离子进程与超时，不适合再叠加并发）。
        _pass_args = []
        if opts.get("rules_dir"):
            _pass_args += ["--rules-dir", str(opts["rules_dir"])]
        if policy_f:
            _pass_args += ["--policy", str(policy_f)]
        if trust_f:
            _pass_args += ["--trust", str(trust_f)]
        _skills_n = len(find_skills(path))
        _workers = 1 if opts.get("dynamic") else batch_workers(_skills_n)
        if _workers > 1:
            print(f"⚙️ 批量并行：{_skills_n} 个目标，并发 {_workers}（进程级；动态取证强制串行）",
                  file=sys.stderr)
        results = analyze_dir(path, osv=osv, passthrough=_pass_args,
                              dynamic=bool(opts.get("dynamic")))
        # v3.2.0：批量扫描后对高危 Skill 自动启用动态沙箱取证（不再需用户手动选择 --dynamic）
        # —— 该阶段**始终串行**，避免并发叠加动态取证带来的资源竞争与不确定性。
        results = auto_dynamic_review(results, osv=osv)
        valid = [r for r in results if "error" not in r]
        if as_json:
            print(json.dumps(results, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str))
        else:
            print(render_summary_table(results))
            print("\n---\n")
            print(render_nine_dim_table(results))
            flagged = [r for r in valid if verdict_label(r) != "✅ 未发现风险"]
            if flagged:
                print("\n---\n### 需关注的 Skill 详情\n")
                for r in flagged:
                    print(render_markdown(r))
                    print()
            else:
                print("\n全部 Skill 未发现风险。")
        if sarif and valid:
            export_sarif(valid, Path(sarif))
            print(f"SARIF 已导出（合并 {len(valid)} 个 Skill）：{sarif}", file=sys.stderr)
        if sbom and valid:
            export_sbom(valid, Path(sbom))
            print(f"SBOM 已导出（合并 {len(valid)} 个 Skill）：{sbom}", file=sys.stderr)
        worst = min((sev_order.get(h["sev"], 9) for r in valid for h in collect_all_hits(r)), default=9)
        if worst <= threshold:
            exit_code = 2
    else:
        result = analyze(path, osv=osv, dynamic=opts.get("dynamic", False))
        if "error" in result:
            # v2.6.2 明确判定：错误路径也输出结构化的结论，而非裸错误文本。
            # v3.3.3 修复（接口契约）：--json 模式下错误结果必须输出 **JSON**（含 error/
            # admission_gate/verdict），否则批量并行子进程与 CI 解析器无法解析——此前输出
            # 文本会导致 json.loads 失败（实测：目标不存在 + --json 输出非 JSON）。
            if as_json:
                print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str))
            else:
                print(f"❌ {result['error']}")
                print(f"【最终判定】{result.get('admission_gate', 'ERROR（无法完成扫描）')}"
                      f"｜{result.get('verdict', '扫描失败，请检查目标路径是否为有效 Skill 目录。')}")
            for w in result.get("warnings", []):
                print(f"  - {w}", file=sys.stderr)
            sys.exit(1)
        if sarif:
            export_sarif(result, Path(sarif))
            print(f"SARIF 已导出：{sarif}", file=sys.stderr)
        if sbom:
            export_sbom(result, Path(sbom))
            print(f"SBOM 已导出：{sbom}", file=sys.stderr)
        if as_json:
            print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str))
        else:
            print(render_markdown(result))
        worst = min((sev_order.get(h["sev"], 9) for h in collect_all_hits(result)), default=9)
        if worst <= threshold:
            exit_code = 2
    sys.exit(exit_code)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
